I mean… I’ve been busy with all of it, but not like I have to do some specific thing over and over or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling anxious about the fundraiser or your responsibilities in general?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe a little stressed, but I wouldn’t say I’ve really thought of it as anxiety.
